2026-11-27T15:24:17.9069139Z ##[start-action display=Set Trivy environment variables;id=__aquasecurity_trivy-action.__run_3]
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0804749Z ##[group]Run # Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0805884Z [36;1m# Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0806774Z [36;1m# This limitation affects how we handle default values and empty inputs.[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0807491Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0808021Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0808509Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0809090Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0809350Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0809649Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0809999Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0810306Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0810600Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0811152Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0812054Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0812950Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0813931Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't[0m
2026-11-27T15:24:18.0814581Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m

2026-11-27T15:25:44.8710756Z ##[start-action display=Set Trivy environment variables;id=__aquasecurity_trivy-action_2.__run_3]
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8727724Z ##[group]Run # Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8728655Z [36;1m# Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8729379Z [36;1m# This limitation affects how we handle default values and empty inputs.[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8729986Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8730437Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8730860Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8731359Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8731592Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8731858Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8732161Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8732424Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8732666Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8733137Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8734117Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8734854Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8735666Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8736329Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8736731Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided() {[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8737058Z [36;1m  local var_name="$1"[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8737362Z [36;1m  local input_value="$2"[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8737673Z [36;1m  local default_value="$3"[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8737977Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-27T15:25:44.8738323Z [36;1m  if [ -n "$input_value" ] && [ "$input_value" != "$default_value" ]; then[0m
